# A57 — Session, cookie, archive and import rules against Android

Static, read-only audit, 2026-10-15. Android evidence is pinned to **b3db90ebf2ae957d554f1e2560da22d03fe20e7e**, read with `git show` in `/Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane`; the checked-out Android HEAD was `b389d503ce62248daa29ef02cd05594fb0f93b5c` when checked and is not the evidence revision. iOS is `/Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish`, HEAD **76473fc9571798070c0d11ed7cbdc157ead222a9**. `KudosTests/…` resolves there; `android/…` resolves in the pinned Android tree.

Binding: [AO3_NETWORKING_POLICY.md](../../AO3_NETWORKING_POLICY.md) and [DATA_AND_PERSISTENCE_INVARIANTS.md](../../DATA_AND_PERSISTENCE_INVARIANTS.md). Same source-comparison method as A52–A56: a kept verdict concerns the reader guarantee, not matching class names. No build, test execution, branch switch, sign-in, AO3 contact, commit, push, or TASKS edit. Related Android test names are source references, not passing test results. An absent test alone is not a missing feature.

<!-- AUDIT_SUMMARY -->

| `cookieHeadersAreScopedAndExpiredCookiesAreDropped`<br>Cookie Headers Are Scoped And Expired Cookies Are Dropped. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:28` <code>#expect(session.hasSessionCookie)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:29` <code>#expect(session.cookieHeader(for: workURL) == "work=allowed; _otwarchive_session=session")</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:30` <code>#expect(session.cookieHeader(for: homeURL) == "_otwarchive_session=session")</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:31` <code>#expect(session.cookieHeader(for: externalURL) == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:32` <code>#expect(session.cookieHeader(for: similarPathURL) == "_otwarchive_session=session")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3CookieJar.kt:10–19` <code>fun cookieHeader(session: AO3Session, url: String): String? { val parsed = url.toHttpUrlOrNull() ?: return null if (!parsed.isHttps &#124;&#124; !isAO3Host(parsed.host)) return null val pairs = session.validCookies(clock) .filter { it.appliesTo(url, clock) } .sortedByDescending { it.path.length } .map { it.toCookieHeaderPair() } return pairs.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.joinToString("; ")</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3Session.kt:24–38` <code>fun isExpired(clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()): Boolean { return expiresAtEpochMillis?.let { it &lt;= clock.millis() } ?: false } fun appliesTo(url: String, clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()): Boolean { if (isExpired(clock)) return false val parsed = url.toHttpUrlOrNull() ?: return false val normalizedDomain = domain.trim().lowercase().trimStart('.') val requestHost = parsed.host.lowercase() val hostMatches = requestHost == normalizedDomain &#124;&#124; requestHost.endsWith(".$normalizedDomain") val requestPath = parsed.encodedPath.ifBlank { "/" } val pathMatches = requestPath == path &#124;&#124; (requestPath.startsWith(path) &amp;&amp; (path.endsWith("/") &#124;&#124; requestPath.drop(path.length).startsWith("/"))) val schemeMatches = !isSecure &#124;&#124; parsed.isHttps return hostMatches &amp;&amp; pathMatches &amp;&amp; schemeMatches</code> | AO3AuthTest.attachesCookiesOnlyForSecureAo3HostsAndMatchingPaths covers HTTPS, expiry, off-host and path boundaries. |
| `sessionRoundTripsThroughCodable`<br>Session Round Trips Through Codable. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:43` <code>#expect(restored == original)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3Session.kt:113–125` <code>@Serializable data class AO3Session( val username: String, val cookies: List&lt;AO3StoredCookie&gt;, val savedAtEpochMillis: Long = Clock.systemUTC().millis() ) { fun validCookies(clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()): List&lt;AO3StoredCookie&gt; { return cookies.filter { !it.isExpired(clock) &amp;&amp; AO3StoredCookie.isAO3Domain(it.domain) } } fun hasSessionCookie(clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()): Boolean { return validCookies(clock).any { it.name == AO3StoredCookie.SessionCookieName } }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3SessionStore.kt:47–57` <code>private val json: Json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true // Persist every field, including ones equal to their default. // `savedAtEpochMillis` defaults to `Clock.systemUTC().millis()`, and // kotlinx.serialization re-evaluates that default when deciding whether // to omit it — so a session constructed and written inside the same // millisecond had the field silently dropped from the file, and a later // load re-stamped it with load time instead. | `loggedInHTMLAndUsernameAreRecognized`<br>Logged In HTMLAnd Username Are Recognized. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:113` <code>#expect(LiveAO3SessionValidator.isLoggedIn(html: html))</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:114` <code>#expect(LiveAO3SessionValidator.username(in: html) == "Example User")</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:115` <code>#expect(!LiveAO3SessionValidator.isLoggedIn(html: "&lt;body class='logged-out'&gt;&lt;/body&gt;"))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3UsernameParser.kt:20–37` <code>fun isLoggedIn(html: String): Boolean { val document = Jsoup.parse(html, AO3Constants.BASE_URL) if (document.body().hasClass("logged-in")) return true return document.select("a[href=/users/logout], form[action=/users/logout]").isNotEmpty() } fun username(html: String): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html, AO3Constants.BASE_URL) document.select("#greeting a[href^=/users/]").forEach { link -&gt; val href = link.attr("href") if (!href.startsWith("/users/") &#124;&#124; href.endsWith("/login") &#124;&#124; href.endsWith("/logout")) { return@forEach } val encoded = href.removePrefix("/users/").substringBefore("/") val decoded = URLDecoder.decode(encoded, StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name()).trim() if (decoded.isNotBlank()) return decoded } return null</code> | AO3SessionRestoreTest.validLoggedInFixtureKeepsSession / loggedOutFixtureExpiresSession. |

AO3SessionGenerationGuardTest.fileSessionStoreDeleteReturnValueReflectsSuccess covers the Boolean delete contract. |
| `installNeverWritesToTheSharedHTTPCookieStorage`<br>Install Never Writes To The Shared HTTPCookie Storage. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:286` <code>#expect(HTTPCookieStorage.shared.cookies?.contains { $0.name == marker } != true)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3CookieStore.kt:32–39` <code>override suspend fun install(session: AO3Session) { cookieManager.setAcceptCookie(true) session.validCookies().forEach { cookie -&gt; // toCookieManagerSetCookieHeader writes Path/Domain/Secure/HttpOnly/Expires. cookieManager.setCookie(AO3Constants.BASE_URL, cookie.toCookieManagerSetCookieHeader()) } cookieManager.flush() }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:88–107` <code>fun defaultOkHttpClient(config: AO3NetworkConfig = AO3NetworkConfig()): OkHttpClient { return OkHttpClient.Builder() .installDemoNetworkBlock() // OkHttp sends a request again on its own (a 408, a dropped pooled connection), // a POST included, outside the pacing and the session check: a write could reach // AO3 twice for one tap (A50 F1). Only `AO3RetryPolicy` retries, and only a GET. .retryOnConnectionFailure(false) // A 503 saying "Retry-After: 0" is sent again by OkHttp itself whatever the line // above says, outside the pacing (A51 F1). As 1 second it is left to // AO3RetryPolicy, which paces it and never retries a write. .addNetworkInterceptor { chain -&gt; val response = chain.proceed(chain.request()) if (response.code == 503 &amp;&amp; response.header("Retry-After")?.trim() == "0") response.newBuilder().header("Retry-After", "1").build() else response } .callTimeout(config.callTimeoutSeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS) .followRedirects(false) .followSslRedirects(false) .addInterceptor(AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor()) .build()</code> | Install writes Android CookieManager only; the default OkHttp builder has no bridge CookieJar. AO3AuthTest covers header construction; no direct jar-isolation test located. |

| `recognizesLoggedInHeaderFixture`<br>Recognizes Logged In Header Fixture. | `KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:339` <code>#expect(LiveAO3SessionValidator.isLoggedIn(html: html))</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3AuthTests.swift:340` <code>#expect(LiveAO3SessionValidator.username(in: html) == "AO3_Reader")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/account/AO3UsernameParser.kt:20–37` <code>fun isLoggedIn(html: String): Boolean { val document = Jsoup.parse(html, AO3Constants.BASE_URL) if (document.body().hasClass("logged-in")) return true return document.select("a[href=/users/logout], form[action=/users/logout]").isNotEmpty() } fun username(html: String): String? { val document = Jsoup.parse(html, AO3Constants.BASE_URL) document.select("#greeting a[href^=/users/]").forEach { link -&gt; val href = link.attr("href") if (!href.startsWith("/users/") &#124;&#124; href.endsWith("/login") &#124;&#124; href.endsWith("/logout")) { return@forEach } val encoded = href.removePrefix("/users/").substringBefore("/") val decoded = URLDecoder.decode(encoded, StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name()).trim() if (decoded.isNotBlank()) return decoded } return null</code> | AO3SessionRestoreTest.validLoggedInFixtureKeepsSession / loggedOutFixtureExpiresSession. |

As 1 second it is left to // AO3RetryPolicy, which paces it and never retries a write. .addNetworkInterceptor { chain -&gt; val response = chain.proceed(chain.request()) if (response.code == 503 &amp;&amp; response.header("Retry-After")?.trim() == "0") response.newBuilder().header("Retry-After", "1").build() else response } .callTimeout(config.callTimeoutSeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS) .followRedirects(false) .followSslRedirects(false) .addInterceptor(AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor()) .build()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3CookieStore.kt:32–39` <code>override suspend fun install(session: AO3Session) { cookieManager.setAcceptCookie(true) session.validCookies().forEach { cookie -&gt; // toCookieManagerSetCookieHeader writes Path/Domain/Secure/HttpOnly/Expires. cookieManager.setCookie(AO3Constants.BASE_URL, cookie.toCookieManagerSetCookieHeader()) } cookieManager.flush() }</code> | HTTPCookieStorage.shared and its pre-fix Apple migration do not exist on Android; CookieManager is separate from the default OkHttp client. |

## AO3RedirectCookieRelayTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |

| `replacesOnlyTheSessionCookiesValueLeavingOthersUntouched`<br>Replaces Only The Session Cookies Value Leaving Others Untouched. | `KudosTests/AO3RedirectCookieRelayTests.swift:28` <code>#expect(merged != nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3RedirectCookieRelayTests.swift:29` <code>#expect(merged?.contains("_otwarchive_session=fresh-value-with-flash") == true)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3RedirectCookieRelayTests.swift:30` <code>#expect(merged?.contains("stale-value") == false)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3RedirectCookieRelayTests.swift:31` <code>#expect(merged?.contains("other_cookie=unchanged") == true)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RedirectCookieRelay.kt:87–115` <code>fun mergedCookieHeader( currentHeader: String?, setCookieHeaders: List&lt;String&gt;, responseUrl: HttpUrl? ): String? { if (currentHeader.isNullOrEmpty() &#124;&#124; responseUrl == null) return null val updated = setCookieHeaders .mapNotNull { line -&gt; Cookie.parse(responseUrl, line) } .firstOrNull { it.name == SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } ?: return null val pairs = currentHeader .split("; ") .mapNotNull { pair -&gt; val index = pair.indexOf('=') if (index &lt;= 0) return@mapNotNull null val name = pair.substring(0, index) val value = pair.substring(index + 1) name to value } .toMutableList() val existingIndex = pairs.indexOfFirst { it.first == updated.name } if (existingIndex &gt;= 0) { pairs[existingIndex] = updated.name to updated.value } else { pairs.add(updated.name to updated.value) } return pairs.joinToString("; ") { "${it.first}=${it.second}" }</code> | AO3RedirectCookieRelayTest.replacesOnlyTheSessionCookiesValueLeavingOthersUntouched covers the corresponding pure decision; real relay wiring is also tested. |

| `validatorRedirectDoesNotForwardTheSessionCookie`<br>Validator Redirect Does Not Forward The Session Cookie. | `KudosTests/LiveAO3SessionValidatorRelayTests.swift:40` <code>#expect(originHits.value == 1, "the loopback origin was never reached")</code><br>`KudosTests/LiveAO3SessionValidatorRelayTests.swift:41` <code>#expect( attackerHits.value == 1, "the redirect never reached the attacker — test proved nothing" )</code><br>`KudosTests/LiveAO3SessionValidatorRelayTests.swift:46` <code>#expect(attackerCookie == nil &#124;&#124; attackerCookie?.isEmpty == true)</code><br>`KudosTests/LiveAO3SessionValidatorRelayTests.swift:47` <code>#expect(attackerCookie?.contains("SECRET-SESSION") != true)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/auth/AO3SessionValidator.kt:43–49` <code>override suspend fun validate(session: AO3Session): AO3SessionValidation { val cookieHeader = cookieJar.cookieHeader(session, validationUrl) ?: return AO3SessionValidation.Expired return when (val result = client.get(validationUrl, mapOf("Cookie" to cookieHeader))) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; mapFailure(result.error) is AO3Result.Success -&gt; mapSuccess(session, result.value)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:104–107` <code>.followRedirects(false) .followSslRedirects(false) .addInterceptor(AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor()) .build()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor.kt:75–87` <code>val action = AO3RedirectCookieRelay.redirectCookieAction( currentHeader = request.header("Cookie"), setCookieHeaders = userResponse.headers("Set-Cookie"), responseUrl = request.url, newRequestUrl = newUrl, isTrusted = isTrusted ) when (action) { AO3RedirectCookieRelay.RedirectCookieAction.LeaveUnchanged -&gt; Unit is AO3RedirectCookieRelay.RedirectCookieAction.Set -&gt; requestBuilder.header("Cookie", action.value) AO3RedirectCookieRelay.RedirectCookieAction.Strip -&gt; requestBuilder.removeHeader("Cookie")</code> | Validator delegates to the same relay-equipped client; the off-host Cookie is stripped. AO3RedirectCookieRelayTest.stripsCookieWhenRedirectLeavesTrustedHost is an integration test of that stack; no validator-specific integration test located. |

## AO3ClientPolicyTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `rateLimitedHonorsRetryAfterWhenLargerThanBackoff`<br>Rate Limited Honors Retry After When Larger Than Backoff. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:12` <code>#expect(delay == 10)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RetryPolicy.kt:36–42` <code>fun retryDelayMillis(error: AO3Error, retryNumber: Int): Long { val backoff = 500L * (1L shl (retryNumber - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)) return when (error) { is AO3Error.RateLimited -&gt; maxOf(error.retryAfterMillis ?: 0L, backoff) is AO3Error.Overloaded -&gt; maxOf(error.retryAfterMillis ?: 0L, backoff) else -&gt; backoff }</code> | AO3NetworkingCoreTest.retryDelayUsesBackoffAndHonorsRetryAfterMinimum. |

| `firstRequestPaysNoWaitButClaimsTheNextSlot`<br>First Request Pays No Wait But Claims The Next Slot. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:89` <code>#expect(step.wait &lt;= 0)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:90` <code>#expect(step.nextAllowed == now.addingTimeInterval(0.6))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoordinator.kt:18–34` <code>suspend fun &lt;T&gt; coordinate(operation: suspend () -&gt; T): T { return semaphore.withPermit { awaitSpacingTurn() operation() } } private suspend fun awaitSpacingTurn() { spacingMutex.withLock { val now = clock.nowMillis() if (hasStartedRequest) { val waitMillis = (nextRequestStartMillis - now).coerceAtLeast(0) if (waitMillis &gt; 0) delay.delay(waitMillis) } hasStartedRequest = true nextRequestStartMillis = clock.nowMillis() + config.minDelayBetweenRequestsMillis }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3NetworkConfig.kt:3–8` <code>data class AO3NetworkConfig( val maxConcurrentRequests: Int = 3, // Apple paces at 0.6s between starts (AO3RequestDefaults). Keep parity. val minDelayBetweenRequestsMillis: Long = 600, val maxRetries: Int = 2, val callTimeoutSeconds: Long = 30</code> | AO3NetworkingCoreTest.appliesMinimumDelayBetweenRequestStarts. Mutex holds across the wait, measuring the next start from the actual clock, so idle gaps do not accumulate credit. |

| `anonymousSessionConfigurationUsesAPrivateEphemeralCookieJarNeverTheSharedOne`<br>Anonymous Session Configuration Uses APrivate Ephemeral Cookie Jar Never The Shared One. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:123` <code>#expect(config.httpCookieStorage !== HTTPCookieStorage.shared)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:124` <code>#expect(config.httpShouldSetCookies == true)</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:125` <code>#expect(config.httpCookieAcceptPolicy == .always)</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:88–107` <code>fun defaultOkHttpClient(config: AO3NetworkConfig = AO3NetworkConfig()): OkHttpClient { return OkHttpClient.Builder() .installDemoNetworkBlock() // OkHttp sends a request again on its own (a 408, a dropped pooled connection), // a POST included, outside the pacing and the session check: a write could reach // AO3 twice for one tap (A50 F1). Only `AO3RetryPolicy` retries, and only a GET. .retryOnConnectionFailure(false) // A 503 saying "Retry-After: 0" is sent again by OkHttp itself whatever the line // above says, outside the pacing (A51 F1). As 1 second it is left to // AO3RetryPolicy, which paces it and never retries a write. .addNetworkInterceptor { chain -&gt; val response = chain.proceed(chain.request()) if (response.code == 503 &amp;&amp; response.header("Retry-After")?.trim() == "0") response.newBuilder().header("Retry-After", "1").build() else response } .callTimeout(config.callTimeoutSeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS) .followRedirects(false) .followSslRedirects(false) .addInterceptor(AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor()) .build()</code> | Android does not maintain an anonymous Cloudflare CookieJar; default OkHttp uses no ambient account-cookie storage. There is no Apple private jar to filter/purge. This preserves account-cookie isolation but lacks iOS challenge-cookie warming. |

As 1 second it is left to // AO3RetryPolicy, which paces it and never retries a write. .addNetworkInterceptor { chain -&gt; val response = chain.proceed(chain.request()) if (response.code == 503 &amp;&amp; response.header("Retry-After")?.trim() == "0") response.newBuilder().header("Retry-After", "1").build() else response } .callTimeout(config.callTimeoutSeconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS) .followRedirects(false) .followSslRedirects(false) .addInterceptor(AO3RedirectCookieRelayInterceptor()) .build()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3PageCache.kt:10–21` <code>/** iOS AO3AuthorPageCache: process memory only, 5 minutes fresh / 24 hours fallback, 128 pages. */ class AO3PageCache( private val now: () -&gt; Long = System::currentTimeMillis, private val ttlMillis: Long = 5 * 60 * 1000L, private val staleMillis: Long = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L, private val maxEntries: Int = 128 ) { enum class Kind { Dashboard, About, Works, AuthorSeries, Bookmarks, SeriesDetail, Inbox, Stats } data class Scope(val viewer: String?, val generation: Int?) data class Key(val url: String, val scope: Scope, val kind: Kind) private data class Entry(val response: AO3HttpResponse, val freshUntil: Long, val staleUntil: Long) private val entries = mutableMapOf&lt;Key, Entry&gt;()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3PageCache.kt:111–112` <code>@Synchronized fun clear() { removed { true }; entries.clear() }</code> | Android has no general anonymous HTTP response cache; its separate parsed-page memory cache is cleared explicitly. | `cancelledCallersDoNotBorrowPacerTime`<br>Cancelled Callers Do Not Borrow Pacer Time. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:281` <code>#expect(worstWait &lt;= 0.6)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoalescer.kt:55–60` <code>suspend fun coalesce( key: K, operation: suspend () -&gt; V ): V { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() if (currentCoroutineContext()[AO3UncoalescedRead] != null) return operation()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:184–189` <code>while (true) { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() val result = coordinator.coordinate { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() beforeSend() performOnce(method, url, headers, formBody)</code> | Already-cancelled coalesced GETs stop before borrowing time. AO3NetworkingCoreTest.lastWaiterLeavingDuringPacingPreventsDispatch covers later cancellation. |
| `cancelledDownloadsDoNotBorrowPacerTime`<br>Cancelled Downloads Do Not Borrow Pacer Time. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:306` <code>#expect(worstWait &lt;= 0.6)</code> | **unsure** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:226–242` <code>private suspend fun executeBinaryWithRetry( method: AO3HttpMethod, url: String, headers: Map&lt;String, String&gt; ): AO3Result&lt;AO3BinaryResponse&gt; { var retryNumber = 0 while (true) { val result = coordinator.coordinate { performBinaryOnce(method, url, headers) } if (result is AO3Result.Success) return result val error = (result as AO3Result.Failure).error retryNumber += 1 if (!retryPolicy.shouldRetry(method, error, retryNumber)) return result delay.delay(retryPolicy.retryDelayMillis(error, retryNumber))</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoordinator.kt:18–34` <code>suspend fun &lt;T&gt; coordinate(operation: suspend () -&gt; T): T { return semaphore.withPermit { awaitSpacingTurn() operation() } } private suspend fun awaitSpacingTurn() { spacingMutex.withLock { val now = clock.nowMillis() if (hasStartedRequest) { val waitMillis = (nextRequestStartMillis - now).coerceAtLeast(0) if (waitMillis &gt; 0) delay.delay(waitMillis) } hasStartedRequest = true nextRequestStartMillis = clock.nowMillis() + config.minDelayBetweenRequestsMillis }</code> | Binary GET path has no explicit ensureActive before spacing; suspended semaphore/mutex/delay are cancellable, but the exact already-cancelled fast-path pacing assertion has no located test. Do not infer 50 queued slots from this source alone. |
| `aCancelledLoadIsNeverRetried`<br>A Cancelled Load Is Never Retried. | `KudosTests/AO3ClientPolicyTests.swift:313` <code>#expect(AO3Client.retryDelay(for: URLError(.cancelled), attempt: 1) == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:219–223` <code>} catch (error: CancellationException) { throw error } catch (error: IOException) { AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.networkFromTransport(error)) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3Client.kt:403–418` <code>private suspend fun Call.await(): Response { return suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation -&gt; continuation.invokeOnCancellation { cancel() } enqueue(object : Callback { override fun onFailure(call: Call, e: IOException) { if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resumeWithException(e) } override fun onResponse(call: Call, response: Response) { if (continuation.isActive) { continuation.resume(response) } else { response.close() } } })</code> | AO3NetworkingCoreTest.lastWaiterLeavingDuringBackoffPreventsRetry; caller cancellation is propagated, never transformed into a retried GET. |

## AO3RequestCoordinatorTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `neverExceedsTheLimit`<br>Never Exceeds The Limit. | `KudosTests/AO3RequestCoordinatorTests.swift:40` <code>#expect(peak &lt;= limit) // the safety bound</code><br>`KudosTests/AO3RequestCoordinatorTests.swift:41` <code>#expect(peak &gt;= 2) // and it really did run in parallel</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoordinator.kt:8–34` <code>class AO3RequestCoordinator( private val config: AO3NetworkConfig = AO3NetworkConfig(), private val clock: AO3Clock = SystemAO3Clock, private val delay: AO3Delay = CoroutineAO3Delay ) { private val semaphore = Semaphore(config.maxConcurrentRequests) private val spacingMutex = Mutex() private var hasStartedRequest = false private var nextRequestStartMillis = 0L suspend fun &lt;T&gt; coordinate(operation: suspend () -&gt; T): T { return semaphore.withPermit { awaitSpacingTurn() operation() } } private suspend fun awaitSpacingTurn() { spacingMutex.withLock { val now = clock.nowMillis() if (hasStartedRequest) { val waitMillis = (nextRequestStartMillis - now).coerceAtLeast(0) if (waitMillis &gt; 0) delay.delay(waitMillis) } hasStartedRequest = true nextRequestStartMillis = clock.nowMillis() + config.minDelayBetweenRequestsMillis }</code> | Coroutine Semaphore.withPermit bounds concurrency and releases in finally, including exceptional completion; suspended acquisition is cancellable. AO3NetworkingCoreTest.neverExceedsConfiguredConcurrency covers the bound; no exact test located for every failure/cancel variant. |
| `limitOfOneSerializes`<br>Limit Of One Serializes. | `KudosTests/AO3RequestCoordinatorTests.swift:61` <code>#expect(await tracker.peak == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoordinator.kt:8–34` <code>class AO3RequestCoordinator( private val config: AO3NetworkConfig = AO3NetworkConfig(), private val clock: AO3Clock = SystemAO3Clock, private val delay: AO3Delay = CoroutineAO3Delay ) { private val semaphore = Semaphore(config.maxConcurrentRequests) private val spacingMutex = Mutex() private var hasStartedRequest = false private var nextRequestStartMillis = 0L suspend fun &lt;T&gt; coordinate(operation: suspend () -&gt; T): T { return semaphore.withPermit { awaitSpacingTurn() operation() } } private suspend fun awaitSpacingTurn() { spacingMutex.withLock { val now = clock.nowMillis() if (hasStartedRequest) { val waitMillis = (nextRequestStartMillis - now).coerceAtLeast(0) if (waitMillis &gt; 0) delay.delay(waitMillis) } hasStartedRequest = true nextRequestStartMillis = clock.nowMillis() + config.minDelayBetweenRequestsMillis }</code> | Coroutine Semaphore.withPermit bounds concurrency and releases in finally, including exceptional completion; suspended acquisition is cancellable. AO3NetworkingCoreTest.neverExceedsConfiguredConcurrency covers the bound; no exact test located for every failure/cancel variant. |

## RequestCoalescerTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `coalescesConcurrentIdenticalRequests`<br>Coalesces Concurrent Identical Requests. | `KudosTests/RequestCoalescerTests.swift:33` <code>#expect(results == Array(repeating: 42, count: 10))</code><br>`KudosTests/RequestCoalescerTests.swift:34` <code>#expect(await counter.count == 1) // ran once despite 10 concurrent callers</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/AO3RequestCoalescer.kt:55–95` <code>suspend fun coalesce( key: K, operation: suspend () -&gt; V ): V { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() if (currentCoroutineContext()[AO3UncoalescedRead] != null) return operation() val shared = mutex.withLock { val entry = inFlight[key]?.takeUnless { it.result.isCompleted } ?: InFlight&lt;V&gt;().also { created -&gt; // Register the first waiter before this job can start, even on an immediate dispatcher. created.job = scope.launch(start = CoroutineStart.LAZY) { try { created.result.complete(operation()) } catch (error: Throwable) { created.result.completeExceptionally(error) } } created.job.invokeOnCompletion { error -&gt; // A cancelled scope can stop a lazy job before its body ever runs. if (error != null) created.result.completeExceptionally(error) } inFlight[key] = created } entry.waiters += 1 entry } try { currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive() shared.job.start() return shared.result.await() } finally { // Cancellation must not interrupt unregistering the last waiter. withContext(NonCancellable) { mutex.withLock { shared.waiters -= 1 if (shared.waiters == 0) { if (inFlight[key] === shared) inFlight.remove(key) shared.job.cancel() } } }</code> | AO3NetworkingCoreTest.identicalConcurrentRequestsShareOneOperationAndThenEvict / differentKeysDoNotCoalesce / cancellingOneWaiterLeavesTheSharedOperationForTheOther / lastWaiterLeavingCancelsTheOperationBeforeItsFetch. Waiter registration/unregistration is once per try/finally; a completed result is not reused. |

| `futureDatedArchiveTimestampsAreClampedAtDecode`<br>A forged future timestamp cannot gain unbounded merge priority (Android rejects rather than clamps). | `KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:46` <code>#expect(json.contains("3000-01-01"), "fixture did not actually carry a future date")</code><br>`KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:56` <code>#expect(manifest.exportedAt &lt;= ceiling, "exportedAt escaped the decode clamp")</code><br>`KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:58` <code>#expect(work.dateAdded &lt;= ceiling, "dateAdded escaped the clamp")</code><br>`KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:59` <code>#expect(work.lastModifiedAt.map { $0 &lt;= ceiling } ?? true, "lastModifiedAt escaped the clamp")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupValidator.kt:25–39` <code>fun decodeManifest(bytes: ByteArray): KudosBackupManifest { if (bytes.size &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES) { throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(BackupPaths.MANIFEST) } val json = bytes.toString(Charsets.UTF_8) val manifest = try { BackupJson.decodeFromString&lt;KudosBackupManifest&gt;(json) } catch (error: SerializationException) { throw BackupError.InvalidJson(error) } catch (error: IllegalArgumentException) { throw BackupError.InvalidJson(error) } return validateManifest(manifest)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupValidator.kt:170–189` <code>fun parseInstant( value: String, field: String, exportedAt: Instant? = null, now: Instant = Instant.now() ): Instant { val parsed = try { Instant.parse(value) } catch (_: DateTimeParseException) { try { OffsetDateTime.parse(value).toInstant() } catch (_: DateTimeParseException) { throw BackupError.InvalidDate(field, value) } } val maxAllowed = now.plus(24, ChronoUnit.HOURS) if (parsed.isAfter(maxAllowed)) { throw BackupError.InvalidDate(field, value) } return if (exportedAt != null &amp;&amp; parsed.isAfter(exportedAt)) exportedAt else parsed</code> | Future-date defense is kept by rejecting dates beyond now+24h, rather than iOS’s clamp-and-continue. A year-3000 archive does not import; honest ordering is not flattened. Related test: BackupTimestampClampTest (date validation); exact test name not established. |

No exact test located. |
| `preservedWorkWithAFileIsNeverByteReplacedByARestore`<br>Preserved Work With AFile Is Never Byte Replaced By ARestore. | `KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:124` <code>#expect(onDisk == genuine, "a preserved work's bytes were replaced by a restore")</code><br>`KudosTests/ArchiveTrustBoundaryTests.swift:125` <code>#expect(onDisk != attacker, "attacker bytes reached a preserved work's file")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:178–185` <code>val existingHasFile = targetId in currentEpubIds val mayReplace = mayRestoreEpub(existing, existingHasFile, mode, incomingModifiedAt) // Check only what would be written, and never let bytes that are not a // readable EPUB replace a file or claim one. iOS stages and inspects the // asset the same way and skips an invalid one, leaving the existing // file, `hasEPUB` and preservation as they were. val incomingEpub = (epubFilesById[archivedId] ?: epubFilesById[targetId]) ?.takeIf { mayReplace &amp;&amp; EpubImportMetadata.isReadablePackage(it) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:2755–2785` <code>/** * iOS `KudosBackupService.mayReplaceEPUB`: whether a restore may overwrite a local work's EPUB * with an archived one. A new record has nothing to lose and a work with no file can always be * filled in. A preserved work that still has its file is never replaced: preservation is the * promise that this exact copy is kept, often of a work that no longer exists upstream. * Otherwise only a newer archive replaces it. */ internal fun mayReplaceEpub( hasLocalFile: Boolean, isPreserved: Boolean, isNewRecord: Boolean, incomingIsNewer: Boolean ): Boolean = isNewRecord &#124;&#124; !hasLocalFile &#124;&#124; (!isPreserved &amp;&amp; incomingIsNewer) /** Shared merge/install policy, including iOS File Merge's add-only overlap rule. */ internal fun mayRestoreEpub( local: SavedWork?, hasLocalFile: Boolean, mode: BackupImportMode, incomingModifiedAt: Instant? ): Boolean = if (mode == BackupImportMode.MERGE &amp;&amp; local != null &amp;&amp; !local.isDeleted) { !hasLocalFile } else { mayReplaceEpub( hasLocalFile = hasLocalFile, isPreserved = local?.epubPreservationStatusRaw == "preserved", isNewRecord = local == null &#124;&#124; mode == BackupImportMode.REPLACE_LIBRARY, incomingIsNewer = local != null &amp;&amp; SyncMerge.shouldApplyIncoming( local.effectiveLastModifiedAt, incomingModifiedAt ) )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupRepository.kt:463–468` <code>val mode = if (applied.mode == BackupImportMode.REPLACE_LIBRARY &amp;&amp; (before != captured &#124;&#124; live != expected) ) BackupImportMode.RECONCILE else applied.mode val allowed = !removedMeanwhile &amp;&amp; !deletedMeanwhile &amp;&amp; mayRestoreEpub( policyLocal, hasFile, mode, applied.epubIncomingModifiedAtByWorkId[workId] )</code> | Preserved local EPUBs are protected in reconcile/merge, including actual imported ZIPs. Explicit Replace treats a record as new and may overwrite by user choice. IncomingEpubGateTest.aPreservedWorkThatStillHasItsFileIsNeverReplaced; SyncRepositoryTest.aFalseFlagCannotHideAPreservedLocalFileFromTheRestoreGate. |

Persist precisely those retractions; retain a deletion // made after capture, whose clock/signature no longer equals it. val retainedIds = snapshot.tombstones.mapTo(mutableSetOf()) { it.id } val retracted = merge.capturedSnapshot.tombstones.filter { it.recordTypeRaw == io.github.cidy02.kudos.core.model.SyncTombstoneRecordType.SAVED_WORK &amp;&amp; it.id !in retainedIds } if (retracted.isNotEmpty()) { val heldById = database.syncTombstoneDao().getAll().associateBy { it.id } for (captured in retracted) { if (heldById[captured.id]?.toDomain() == captured) { database.syncTombstoneDao().deleteById(captured.id) } } } } snapshot.tombstones.forEach { tombstone -&gt; if (tombstone.recordTypeRaw in knownTombstoneTypes) { database.syncTombstoneDao().upsert(tombstone.toEntity()) }</code> | Known-type allow-list gates tombstone persistence; an unknown type is not coerced to savedWork. ## MiniZipCRCTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `theDeclaredCRCIsTheRealCRCOfTheStoredPayload`<br>The Declared CRCIs The Real CRCOf The Stored Payload. | `KudosTests/MiniZipCRCTests.swift:36` <code>#expect(zip.declaredCRC32(named: Self.entryName) == MiniZip.crc32(payload))</code> | **unsure** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupImporter.kt:33–44` <code>try { ZipInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)).use { zip -&gt; while (true) { val entry = zip.nextEntry ?: break val path = entry.name BackupPaths.requireSafeZipEntryName(path) if (entry.isDirectory) { zip.closeEntry() continue } if (!seenEntries.add(path)) throw BackupError.DuplicateEntry(path)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupImporter.kt:193–207` <code>private fun ZipInputStream.readEntryBytes( path: String, maxBytes: Long = BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES ): ByteArray { val output = ByteArrayOutputStream() val buffer = ByteArray(DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE) var total = 0L while (true) { val count = read(buffer) if (count &lt; 0) break total += count if (total &gt; maxBytes) throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(path) output.write(buffer, 0, count) } return output.toByteArray()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/EpubImportMetadata.kt:206–224` <code>fun isReadablePackage(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { // `ZipFile` reads the central directory, as iOS's reader does, and needs a file. val file = File.createTempFile("incoming", ".epub") try { file.writeBytes(bytes) ZipFile(file).use { zip -&gt; | `declaredOversizedUncompressedSizeIsRejectedBeforeAllocation`<br>Declared Oversized Uncompressed Size Is Rejected Before Allocation. | `KudosTests/MiniZipHostileTests.swift:100` <code>#expect(throws: MiniZipError.entryTooLarge) {</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/EpubImportMetadata.kt:206–224` <code>fun isReadablePackage(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { // `ZipFile` reads the central directory, as iOS's reader does, and needs a file. val file = File.createTempFile("incoming", ".epub") try { file.writeBytes(bytes) ZipFile(file).use { zip -&gt; fun text(name: String) = zip.getEntry(name) ?.let { zip.getInputStream(it).use { input -&gt; input.readBounded(MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) } } ?.let(TextDecoding::decode) val packagePath = text("META-INF/container.xml") ?.let { Jsoup.parse(it, "", Parser.xmlParser()) } ?.getAllElements()?.firstOrNull { it.localName() == "rootfile" } ?.attr("full-path") packagePath?.let(::text)?.let(::parseOpf)?.localChapterCount != null } } finally { file.delete() } }.getOrDefault(false)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/EpubImportMetadata.kt:408–428` <code>private fun InputStream.readBounded(limit: Int): ByteArray? { val output = ByteArrayOutputStream() val buffer = ByteArray(DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE) var total = 0 while (true) { val count = read(buffer) if (count &lt; 0) break total += count if (total &gt; limit) return null output.write(buffer, 0, count) } return output.toByteArray() } private fun String.digitsToInt(): Int? = filter(Char::isDigit).takeIf(String::isNotEmpty)?.toIntOrNull() private fun Element.localName(): String = tagName().substringAfter(':').lowercase() // ponytail: bounded header walk; raise only if a legitimate 4,000+ member EPUB appears. private const val MAX_ENTRIES = 4_096 private const val MAX_ENTRY_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024 private const val MAX_TOTAL_METADATA_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024</code> | Only fetched metadata entries are bounded; there is no whole-EPUB declared-size/ratio validation. BackupLimits.MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES) { throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(BackupPaths.MANIFEST) } zip.writeEntry( path = BackupPaths.MANIFEST, bytes = manifestBytes, seenEntries = seenEntries ) checkArchiveSize() manifest.works .sortedBy { it.id } .forEach { work -&gt; val id = BackupPaths.canonicalUuid(work.id, "work.id") val bytes = kudosBackup.epubFilesByWorkId[id] ?: return@forEach zip.writeEntry(BackupPaths.workEntryName(id), bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } var totalFontBytes = 0L manifest.fonts .sortedBy { it.fileName } .forEach { font -&gt; val bytes = kudosBackup.fontFilesByFileName[font.fileName] ?: return@forEach val path = BackupPaths.fontEntryName(font.fileName) if (bytes.size.toLong() &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_FONT_ENTRY_BYTES) throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(path) totalFontBytes += bytes.size if (totalFontBytes &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_TOTAL_FONT_BYTES) { throw BackupError.InvalidPackage("Total font size exceeds limit") } zip.writeEntry(path, bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } kudosBackup.originalFilesByName.toSortedMap().forEach { (name, bytes) -&gt; zip.writeEntry("${BackupPaths.ORIGINALS_DIRECTORY}/$name", bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } } // The central directory is written on close. if (output.size().toLong() &gt; maxArchiveBytes) throw BackupError.ArchiveTooLarge() return output.toByteArray() } private fun ZipOutputStream.writeEntry( path: String, bytes: ByteArray, seenEntries: MutableSet&lt;String&gt; ) { BackupPaths.requireSafeZipEntryName(path) if (!seenEntries.add(path)) throw BackupError.DuplicateEntry(path) if (bytes.size.toLong() &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) { throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(path) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupImporter.kt:33–95` <code>try { ZipInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)).use { zip -&gt; One that is not named as iOS // names them, or is over this device's per-entry limit, is left // out: it must not fail the restore of everything else. val name = path.removePrefix("${BackupPaths.ORIGINALS_DIRECTORY}/") if (BackupPaths.parseOriginalFileName(name) != null) { try { originalFiles[name] = zip.readEntryBytes(path) } catch (_: BackupError.EntryTooLarge) { // Left out. } } zip.closeEntry() continue } val fontFileName = if (path.startsWith("${BackupPaths.FONTS_DIRECTORY}/")) { validateFontEntry(path).also { fileName -&gt; if (!seenFontFileNames.add(BackupPaths.fontFileNameKey(fileName))) { throw BackupError.DuplicateEntry(path) } } } else { null } val payloadLimit = if (fontFileName != null) { BackupLimits.MAX_FONT_ENTRY_BYTES } else { BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES } val payload = zip.readEntryBytes(path, payloadLimit) when { path == BackupPaths.MANIFEST -&gt; manifestBytes = payload path.startsWith("${BackupPaths.WORKS_DIRECTORY}/") -&gt; { val workId = validateWorkEntry(path) epubFiles[workId] = payload } path.startsWith("${BackupPaths.FONTS_DIRECTORY}/") -&gt; { val fileName = requireNotNull(fontFileName) totalFontBytes += payload.size.toLong() if (totalFontBytes &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_TOTAL_FONT_BYTES) { throw BackupError.InvalidPackage("Total font size exceeds limit") } fontFiles[fileName] = payload } else -&gt; Unit } zip.closeEntry()</code> | Android has its own ZIP writer/importer and round-trip tests, but the exact MiniZip two-limit-profile/UTF-8/empty-entry assertion bundle was not proved. BackupLimits.MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES) { throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(BackupPaths.MANIFEST) } zip.writeEntry( path = BackupPaths.MANIFEST, bytes = manifestBytes, seenEntries = seenEntries ) checkArchiveSize() manifest.works .sortedBy { it.id } .forEach { work -&gt; val id = BackupPaths.canonicalUuid(work.id, "work.id") val bytes = kudosBackup.epubFilesByWorkId[id] ?: return@forEach zip.writeEntry(BackupPaths.workEntryName(id), bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } var totalFontBytes = 0L manifest.fonts .sortedBy { it.fileName } .forEach { font -&gt; val bytes = kudosBackup.fontFilesByFileName[font.fileName] ?: return@forEach val path = BackupPaths.fontEntryName(font.fileName) if (bytes.size.toLong() &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_FONT_ENTRY_BYTES) throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(path) totalFontBytes += bytes.size if (totalFontBytes &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_TOTAL_FONT_BYTES) { throw BackupError.InvalidPackage("Total font size exceeds limit") } zip.writeEntry(path, bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } kudosBackup.originalFilesByName.toSortedMap().forEach { (name, bytes) -&gt; zip.writeEntry("${BackupPaths.ORIGINALS_DIRECTORY}/$name", bytes, seenEntries) checkArchiveSize() } } // The central directory is written on close. if (output.size().toLong() &gt; maxArchiveBytes) throw BackupError.ArchiveTooLarge() return output.toByteArray() } private fun ZipOutputStream.writeEntry( path: String, bytes: ByteArray, seenEntries: MutableSet&lt;String&gt; ) { BackupPaths.requireSafeZipEntryName(path) if (!seenEntries.add(path)) throw BackupError.DuplicateEntry(path) if (bytes.size.toLong() &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) { throw BackupError.EntryTooLarge(path) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupPaths.kt:96–112` <code>fun requireSafeZipEntryName(path: String) { if (!isSafeZipEntryName(path)) { throw BackupError.UnsafePath(path) } } fun isSafeZipEntryName(path: String): Boolean { val normalized = path.removeSuffix("/") if (normalized.isBlank()) return false if (normalized.startsWith("/") &#124;&#124; normalized.contains("\\") &#124;&#124; normalized.contains('\u0000')) { return false } val segments = normalized.split("/") return segments.all { segment -&gt; segment.isNotBlank() &amp;&amp; segment != "." &amp;&amp; segment != ".." }</code> | Normal exporter paths are constructed safely, but no equivalent arbitrary-entry writer API/test was established for all empty/duplicate/absolute/traversal inputs. |

| `trailingSlashDirectoryNamedDotDotIsRejected`<br>Trailing Slash Directory Named Dot Dot Is Rejected. | `KudosTests/MiniZipHostileTests.swift:357` <code>#expect(throws: MiniZipError.pathTraversal) {</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/EpubImportMetadata.kt:206–224` <code>fun isReadablePackage(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { // `ZipFile` reads the central directory, as iOS's reader does, and needs a file. val file = File.createTempFile("incoming", ".epub") try { file.writeBytes(bytes) ZipFile(file).use { zip -&gt; fun text(name: String) = zip.getEntry(name) ?.let { zip.getInputStream(it).use { input -&gt; input.readBounded(MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) } } ?.let(TextDecoding::decode) val packagePath = text("META-INF/container.xml") ?.let { Jsoup.parse(it, "", Parser.xmlParser()) } ?.getAllElements()?.firstOrNull { it.localName() == "rootfile" } ?.attr("full-path") packagePath?.let(::text)?.let(::parseOpf)?.localChapterCount != null } } finally { file.delete() } }.getOrDefault(false)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkImporter.kt:201–221` <code>val format = ImportedFileFormat.sniff(bytes, displayName) val finalBytes = when (format) { ImportedFileFormat.EPUB -&gt; bytes ImportedFileFormat.PDF -&gt; pdfConverter.convert(title, bytes) ImportedFileFormat.HTML -&gt; io.github.cidy02.kudos.works.converters.HTMLWorkConverter().convert(title, bytes) ImportedFileFormat.TEXT -&gt; io.github.cidy02.kudos.works.converters.PlainTextWorkConverter().convert(title, bytes) ImportedFileFormat.ZIP -&gt; io.github.cidy02.kudos.works.converters.ArchiveWorkConverter.convert(title, bytes) ImportedFileFormat.UNKNOWN -&gt; null } ?: return WorkImportResult.Failure(null, AO3Error.Validation(unsupportedMessage(format))) // Every path above either passes an EPUB through or builds one, so the // result must be a ZIP by the time it reaches the file store. if (!looksLikeZip(finalBytes)) { return WorkImportResult.Failure( null, AO3Error.Validation("Not a valid EPUB (file is not a ZIP archive).") )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/WorkFileStore.kt:64–68` <code>fun workEpubPath(workId: String): Path { val uuid = UUID.fromString(workId).toString() val path = worksDirectory.resolve("$uuid.epub").normalize() require(path.startsWith(worksDirectory)) { "Unsafe work EPUB path." } return path</code> | EPUB preflight does not enumerate/reject all unsafe names; a valid container/OPF plus an unreferenced hostile path can be accepted. Direct import is weaker still (ZIP magic). UUID-derived destination keeps this importer’s own writes inside works/; no demonstrated outside-file overwrite. Backup ZIP names separately are checked by BackupPaths.requireSafeZipEntryName. |

## MiniZipZip64Tests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `zip64ExtraResolvesSaturatedSizesAndOffset`<br>Zip64 Extra Resolves Saturated Sizes And Offset. | `KudosTests/MiniZipZip64Tests.swift:101` <code>#expect(zip.names == ["a.bin"])</code><br>`KudosTests/MiniZipZip64Tests.swift:102` <code>#expect(zip.data(named: "a.bin") == Data("hello".utf8))</code> | **unsure** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupImporter.kt:33–44` <code>try { ZipInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)).use { zip -&gt; while (true) { val entry = zip.nextEntry ?: break val path = entry.name BackupPaths.requireSafeZipEntryName(path) if (entry.isDirectory) { zip.closeEntry() continue } if (!seenEntries.add(path)) throw BackupError.DuplicateEntry(path)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupImporter.kt:238–252` <code>private fun hasEndOfCentralDirectory(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean { if (bytes.size &lt; 22) return false val firstPossibleOffset = (bytes.size - 22).coerceAtLeast(0) val lastPossibleOffset = (bytes.size - 22 - 65_535).coerceAtLeast(0) for (index in firstPossibleOffset downTo lastPossibleOffset) { if ( bytes[index] == 0x50.toByte() &amp;&amp; bytes[index + 1] == 0x4b.toByte() &amp;&amp; bytes[index + 2] == 0x05.toByte() &amp;&amp; bytes[index + 3] == 0x06.toByte() ) { return true } } return false</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/EpubImportMetadata.kt:206–224` <code>fun isReadablePackage(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { // `ZipFile` reads the central directory, as iOS's reader does, and needs a file. val file = File.createTempFile("incoming", ".epub") try { file.writeBytes(bytes) ZipFile(file).use { zip -&gt; No exact Kotlin regression fixture established; do not infer the malformed ZIP64 cases from ordinary ZIP success. Android’s explicit input/per-entry limits are smaller than iOS’s streaming backup profile. |

## PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `aPromisedButUnreceivedEPUBIsNotRepublishedAsAbsent`<br>A Promised But Unreceived EPUBIs Not Republished As Absent. | `KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(summary.worksMissingPromisedEPUB == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:68` <code>#expect(!work.hasEPUB)</code><br>`KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:69` <code>#expect(work.remoteEPUBPending)</code><br>`KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:77` <code>#expect(republished.manifest.works.first?.hasEPUB == true)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:228–236` <code>// metadata snapshot won (including add-only File Merge). val merged = worksById.getValue(targetId) worksById[targetId] = merged.copy( hasEpub = incomingEpub != null &#124;&#124; existingHasFile, remoteEpubPending = when { incomingEpub != null -&gt; false archived.hasEPUB &amp;&amp; !existingHasFile -&gt; true else -&gt; existing?.remoteEpubPending ?: false }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:130–136` <code>isFavorite = isFavorite, hasGivenKudos = hasGivenKudos, isSaved = isSaved, isQueuedForLater = isQueuedForLater, isFinished = isFinished, hasEPUB = hasEpub &#124;&#124; remoteEpubPending, isComplete = isComplete,</code> | A promised but uninstalled EPUB remains advertised. IncomingEpubGateTest.aPromisedEpubWithMissingOrUnusableBytesIsStillPublished; SyncRepositoryTest.aPromisedEpubThatDidNotArriveIsPublishedByBackupAndSync. |
| `aWorkThatSimplyHasNoEPUBStillPublishesAsAbsent`<br>A Work That Simply Has No EPUBStill Publishes As Absent. | `KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:95` <code>#expect(published.manifest.works.first?.hasEPUB == false)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:130–136` <code>isFavorite = isFavorite, hasGivenKudos = hasGivenKudos, isSaved = isSaved, isQueuedForLater = isQueuedForLater, isFinished = isFinished, hasEPUB = hasEpub &#124;&#124; remoteEpubPending, isComplete = isComplete,</code> | False file and false pending export as false. SyncRepositoryTest.aRemovedDownloadStaysRemovedUnlessAnotherManifestPromisesIt. |
| `receivingTheBytesClearsThePendingFlag`<br>Receiving The Bytes Clears The Pending Flag. | `KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:109` <code>#expect(work.remoteEPUBPending)</code><br>`KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:121` <code>#expect(!work.remoteEPUBPending)</code><br>`KudosTests/PendingAssetIsNotAbsenceTests.swift:122` <code>#expect(!work.epubDigest.isEmpty)</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:228–236` <code>// metadata snapshot won (including add-only File Merge). val merged = worksById.getValue(targetId) worksById[targetId] = merged.copy( hasEpub = incomingEpub != null &#124;&#124; existingHasFile, remoteEpubPending = when { incomingEpub != null -&gt; false archived.hasEPUB &amp;&amp; !existingHasFile -&gt; true else -&gt; existing?.remoteEpubPending ?: false }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupRepository.kt:477–485` <code>val hasInstalledFile = workFileStore.workEpubExists(workId) database.workDao().upsert(entity.copy( hasEpub = hasInstalledFile, remoteEpubPending = when { write is FileWriteResult.Success -&gt; false !hasInstalledFile -&gt; true // A skipped/failed install still owes the promised bytes. else -&gt; entity.remoteEpubPending } ))</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkImporter.kt:280–290` <code>work = epubMetadata.applyTo( restored.copy( isSaved = true, hasEpub = true, remoteEpubPending = false, downloadedAt = restored.downloadedAt ?: downloadedAt, lastModifiedAt = workRepository.currentInstant() ), fillOnly = true ).withImportedAo3Ids() return WorkImportResult.Success(workRepository.upsert(work))</code> | Receiving bytes clears the pending flag, correctly, but these install paths do not compute/persist a digest of the installed file. The iOS nonempty-digest assertion is not kept. Sync uses a fresh on-disk hash instead, reducing the safety impact. IncomingEpubGateTest.aLateEpubClearsThePendingPromiseEvenInFileMerge covers only the flag. |

| `forgedDeletionScheduleCannotHardDeleteAWorkOnTheNextLaunch`<br>Forged Deletion Schedule Cannot Hard Delete AWork On The Next Launch. | `KudosTests/ArchiveDeletionScheduleTests.swift:54` <code>#expect(survivor.isPendingDeletion)</code><br>`KudosTests/ArchiveDeletionScheduleTests.swift:56` <code>#expect(scheduledAt &gt; Date(), "the countdown must be this device's own, not the archive's")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:97–115` <code>fun restoredDeletionState(archivedIsDeleted: Boolean?): RestoredDeletionState { val deleted = archivedIsDeleted == true return RestoredDeletionState( isDeleted = deleted, permanentDeletionScheduledAt = if (deleted) { Instant.now().plus(WorkRepository.RECOVERY_WINDOW) } else { null } ) } /** * iOS `archivedDeletionState`: a record already counting down on this device keeps its * countdown. Restarted every time the deleted flag came back round through sync, as it was, * the date moved out for ever and the record never left Recently Deleted. */ fun keptDeletionSchedule(incoming: Instant?, localIsDeleted: Boolean, local: Instant?): Instant? = if (incoming != null &amp;&amp; localIsDeleted &amp;&amp; local != null) local else incoming</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:793–797` <code>permanentDeletionScheduledAt = keptDeletionSchedule( restored.permanentDeletionScheduledAt, existing.isDeleted, existing.permanentDeletionScheduledAt ),</code> | Incoming deletion starts a local 90-day window, a running local countdown survives, undelete clears it. DeletionCountdownMergeTest.aWorkDeletedOnAnotherDeviceStartsItsCountdownHere / aWorkAlreadyCountingDownKeepsItsCountdownWhenTheDeletionSyncsRoundAgain / aRecordThatIsNoLongerDeletedHasNoCountdown; BackupRestoreSecurityTest covers forged schedules. |

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `theFolderIOSWritesIsTheFixtureAndroidReads`<br>The Folder IOSWrites Is The Fixture Android Reads. | `KudosTests/CrossPlatformFolderSyncTests.swift:75` <code>#expect(try relativeFiles(in: written) == relativeFiles(in: paths.iosFolder))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupPaths.kt:26–43` <code>const val WORKS_DIRECTORY = "Works" const val FONTS_DIRECTORY = "Fonts" /** * iOS `Originals/`: the file a converted import was made from (`&lt;work&gt;.&lt;ext&gt;`) and the * record of its conversion (`&lt;work&gt;.conversion.json`). In a backup archive and in the * sync folder alike. */ const val ORIGINALS_DIRECTORY = "Originals" const val CONVERSION_RECORD_SUFFIX = ".conversion.json" /** iOS `Storage.originalDocumentURL`: the work's UUID in capitals, then the extension. */ fun iosOriginalFileName(workId: String, extension: String): String = "${canonicalUuid(workId, "work.id").uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}.$extension" /** iOS `WorkConversionRecord.url`. */ fun iosConversionRecordFileName(workId: String): String = "${canonicalUuid(workId, "work.id").uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}$CONVERSION_RECORD_SUFFIX"</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:270–291` <code>if (worksDir != null) { // iOS keeps the EPUB of every work the manifest lists, whether or not this // device holds a copy, so one device can never discard an EPUB another // preserved (`FolderSyncService`, after its commit point). manifestOut.works.forEach { expectedWorks.add(BackupPaths.iosEpubAssetIdentifier(it.id)) } val remoteWorks = worksDir.childrenByLowercaseName() snapshot.works.filter { it.hasEpub }.forEach { work -&gt; val localPath = workFileStore.workEpubPath(work.id) if (Files.isRegularFile(localPath)) { // iOS's name: the UUID in capitals. val epubName = BackupPaths.iosEpubAssetIdentifier(work.id) val bytes = Files.readAllBytes(localPath) // The digest of the bytes this device uploads (iOS // `Storage.fileDigest`). Android keeps none of its own, and the // one it carries from another device's manifest can describe an // older copy: a sync-down that trusted it would skip a changed book. epubDigests[BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(work.id)] = BackupPaths.sha256(bytes) writeIfChanged( worksDir, epubName, "application/epub+zip", bytes, existing = remoteWorks[epubName.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)], recase = true )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:600–615` <code>private suspend fun importOriginals(syncDir: DocumentFile, manifest: KudosBackupManifest) { val directory = syncDir.findFile(BackupPaths.ORIGINALS_DIRECTORY) ?: return val listed = manifest.works.mapTo(HashSet()) { BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(it.id) } val inFolder = linkedMapOf&lt;String, MutableList&lt;DocumentFile&gt;&gt;() directory.listFiles().forEach { file -&gt; val name = file.name ?: return@forEach if (name.startsWith(".")) return@forEach val (workId, _) = BackupPaths.parseOriginalFileName(name) ?: return@forEach if (workId in listed) inFolder.getOrPut(workId) { mutableListOf() }.add(file) } val wanted = inFolder.filter { (workId, files) -&gt; val local = workFileStore.originalFile(workId) ?: return@filter true if (workFileStore.conversionRecordExists(workId)) return@filter false val isRecord = { file: DocumentFile -&gt; BackupPaths.parseOriginalFileName(file.name!!)?.second == true } files.any(isRecord) &amp;&amp; files.any { !isRecord(it) &amp;&amp; it.length() == Files.size(local) } }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:625–641` <code>wanted.values.forEach { files -&gt; files.forEach { file -&gt; // The size a provider reports can be zero or unknown, so the stream is limited // too (iOS `KudosBackupContents` limits the bytes it reads); the reported size // still saves reading 128 MB of a file that says it is larger. if (file.length() &gt; BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) return@forEach val bytes = runCatching { context.contentResolver.openInputStream(file.uri)?.use { it.readLimitedBytes(file.name.orEmpty(), BackupLimits.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES) } }.getOrNull() ?: return@forEach batch[file.name!!] = bytes batchBytes += bytes.size } if (batchBytes &gt;= maxEpubBatchBytes) flush() } flush()</code> | UUID case, Works/Originals layout, independent original/record transport and listed-work retention are preserved. SyncRepositoryTest.aFolderIosWroteIsReadAndKept / theFolderAndroidWritesIsTheFixtureIosReads / androidAddsItsWorkToTheIosFolderWithoutPruningEitherDevicesAssets (both writers). Source judgment only; goldens not regenerated. |

A failed re-import must preserve them too: install first. val previous = findOriginal(workId) val temp = Files.createTempFile(originalsDirectory, ".$workId-", ".tmp") try { Files.write(temp, bytes) try { Files.move(temp, destination, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE) } catch (_: IOException) { Files.move(temp, destination, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING) } if (previous != null &amp;&amp; previous != destination) Files.deleteIfExists(previous) Files.deleteIfExists(conversionRecordPath(workId)) FileWriteResult.Success(destination) } finally { Files.deleteIfExists(temp) } } catch (error: Exception) { FileWriteResult.Failure(error.message ?: "Could not archive the original file.", error) } }</code> | Successful HTML original writes retain exact source bytes beside the UUID EPUB. ## ImportedDocumentConverterTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `detectsAO3StyleHTMLRegardlessOfExtension`<br>Detects AO3 Style HTMLRegardless Of Extension. | `KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:52` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: url) == .html)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:28–31` <code>if (bytes.startsWith(PDF_MAGIC)) return PDF val head = bytes.take(1024).toByteArray().decodeToString() if (head.contains("&lt;html", true) &#124;&#124; head.contains("&lt;!doctype html", true)) return HTML</code> | HTML magic wins over .txt. ImportedFileFormatTest covers sniffing; exact test name not established. |
| `detectsPlainTextAndMarkdown`<br>Detects Plain Text And Markdown. | `KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:56` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write("Just words.\n\nMore words.", "txt")) == .plainText)</code><br>`KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:57` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write("Prose, no markup at all.", "md")) == .plainText)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:33–39` <code>return when (fileName?.substringAfterLast('.', "")?.lowercase()) { "epub" -&gt; EPUB "pdf" -&gt; PDF "html", "htm" -&gt; HTML "txt" -&gt; TEXT else -&gt; if (head.isProbablyText()) TEXT else UNKNOWN }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:61–65` <code>/** No NUL bytes and mostly printable — good enough to call it plain text. */ private fun String.isProbablyText(): Boolean { if (isEmpty() &#124;&#124; any { it == '\u0000' }) return false val printable = count { it.isLetterOrDigit() &#124;&#124; it.isWhitespace() &#124;&#124; it in " .,;:!?'\"-()[]{}/\\@#$%^&amp;*+=_&lt;&gt;&#124;~`" } return printable.toDouble() / length &gt;= 0.85</code> | TXT is explicit and printable Markdown falls through to TEXT. ImportedFileFormatTest covers format detection. |
| `detectsEPUBEvenWhenRenamedToZip`<br>Detects EPUBEven When Renamed To Zip. | `KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:67` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write(epub, "zip")) == .epub)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:20–27` <code>fun sniff(bytes: ByteArray, fileName: String?): ImportedFileFormat { val namedEpub = fileName?.endsWith(".epub", ignoreCase = true) == true if (bytes.startsWith(ZIP_MAGIC)) { // The container entry *rescues* a renamed EPUB (`fic.epub.zip`); // it never overrules a file the user already named `.epub`, whose // structure is validated downstream by the ZIP/EPUB check. return if (namedEpub &#124;&#124; looksLikeEpubContainer(bytes)) EPUB else ZIP }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:42–54` <code>/** True when the archive carries `META-INF/container.xml` (the OCF marker). */ private fun looksLikeEpubContainer(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean = runCatching { ZipInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)).use { zip -&gt; // Bounded: the container entry lives at the front of a well-formed // EPUB, so a hostile archive can't make us walk thousands of entries. // ponytail: fixed 64-entry scan, raise only if a real EPUB is missed. repeat(64) { val entry = zip.nextEntry ?: return false if (entry.name.equals("META-INF/container.xml", ignoreCase = true)) return true } false } }.getOrDefault(false)</code> | A renamed EPUB is recognized by META-INF/container.xml. ImportedFileFormatTest covers sniffing; the 64-entry walk is a separate limitation. |
| `detectsFormatsDeferredToLaterTasks`<br>Detects Formats Deferred To Later Tasks. | `KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:71` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write("%PDF-1.7\nstuff", "pdf")) == .pdf)</code><br>`KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:72` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write("{\\rtf1\\ansi text}", "rtf")) == .rtf)</code><br>`KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:73` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write("Rar!\u{1A}\u{07}\u{00}", "rar")) == .rar)</code><br>`KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:78` <code>#expect(ImportedFileFormat.detect(at: try write(mobi, "mobi")) == .mobi)</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:13–14` <code>enum class ImportedFileFormat { EPUB, PDF, HTML, TEXT, ZIP, UNKNOWN;</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/files/ImportedFileFormat.kt:28–39` <code>if (bytes.startsWith(PDF_MAGIC)) return PDF val head = bytes.take(1024).toByteArray().decodeToString() if (head.contains("&lt;html", true) &#124;&#124; head.contains("&lt;!doctype html", true)) return HTML return when (fileName?.substringAfterLast('.', "")?.lowercase()) { "epub" -&gt; EPUB "pdf" -&gt; PDF "html", "htm" -&gt; HTML "txt" -&gt; TEXT else -&gt; if (head.isProbablyText()) TEXT else UNKNOWN }</code> | PDF is recognized, but RTF/RAR/MOBI have no enum/detection branches. RTF/RAR printable data may masquerade as text; binary MOBI becomes UNKNOWN. Exact iOS actionable deferred-format classification is absent. |

| `naturalOrderSortsUnpaddedChapterNumbers`<br>Natural Order Sorts Unpadded Chapter Numbers. | `KudosTests/ImportedDocumentConverterTests.swift:352` <code>#expect(sorted == ["ch-1.txt", "ch-2.txt", "ch-10.txt"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/converters/ArchiveWorkConverter.kt:86–99` <code>/** `chapter2.html` must sort before `chapter10.html`. */ internal object NaturalOrder : Comparator&lt;String&gt; { private val chunk = Regex("\\d+&#124;\\D+") override fun compare(a: String, b: String): Int { val left = chunk.findAll(a.lowercase()).map { it.value }.toList() val right = chunk.findAll(b.lowercase()).map { it.value }.toList() for (i in 0 until minOf(left.size, right.size)) { val l = left[i]; val r = right[i] val ln = l.toLongOrNull(); val rn = r.toLongOrNull() val cmp = if (ln != null &amp;&amp; rn != null) ln.compareTo(rn) else l.compareTo(r) if (cmp != 0) return cmp } return left.size.compareTo(right.size) }</code> | Numeric chunks compare as numbers: chapter 2 precedes 10. No exact Android test located. |

## PDFWorkConverterTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |

## EPUBBuilderTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mimetypeIsTheFirstEntryAndStoredUncompressed`<br>Mimetype Is The First Entry And Stored Uncompressed. | `KudosTests/EPUBBuilderTests.swift:51` <code>#expect(Array(bytes[0..&lt;4]) == [0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04]) // "PK\u{03}\u{04}"</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBBuilderTests.swift:52` <code>#expect(bytes[8] == 0 &amp;&amp; bytes[9] == 0) // compression method: stored</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBBuilderTests.swift:53` <code>#expect(String(bytes: bytes[30..&lt;38], encoding: .utf8) == "mimetype")</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBBuilderTests.swift:54` <code>#expect(String(bytes: bytes[38..&lt;58], encoding: .utf8) == "application/epub+zip")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/converters/EpubBuilder.kt:55–64` <code>// 1. mimetype (must be uncompressed) val mimetype = "application/epub+zip".toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8) val mimeEntry = ZipEntry("mimetype").apply { method = ZipEntry.STORED size = mimetype.size.toLong() crc = CRC32().apply { update(mimetype) }.value } zos.putNextEntry(mimeEntry) zos.write(mimetype) zos.closeEntry()</code> | mimetype is written first with STORED method, actual size and CRC. ## EPUBDigestHonestyTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `aPromisedBookIsNotAssumedToHaveArrived`<br>A Promised Book Is Not Assumed To Have Arrived. | `KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(mine.count == corrected.count)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:63` <code>#expect(mine != corrected)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:121` <code>#expect(localWork.epubDigest == mineDigest)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:122` <code>#expect(try Data(contentsOf: localWork.fileURL) == mine)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:134` <code>#expect(try Data(contentsOf: localWork.fileURL) == corrected)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:135` <code>#expect(localWork.epubDigest == (try digest(of: corrected)))</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:809–814` <code>keepInProgressOverride = archived.keepInProgressOverride ?: existing.keepInProgressOverride, hiddenFromHistoryAt = if (archived.hiddenFromHistoryAt != null) restored.hiddenFromHistoryAt else existing.hiddenFromHistoryAt, datePublished = restored.datePublished.ifBlank { existing.datePublished }, dateUpdated = restored.dateUpdated.ifBlank { existing.dateUpdated }, epubDigest = restored.epubDigest.ifBlank { existing.epubDigest }, assetIdentifier = existing.assetIdentifier.ifEmpty { restored.assetIdentifier },</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:649–653` <code>private fun isUnchangedLocally(work: BackupWork, remote: DocumentFile): Boolean { val local = workFileStore.workEpubPath(work.id) if (!Files.isRegularFile(local) &#124;&#124; Files.size(local) != remote.length()) return false val remoteDigest = work.epubDigest.orEmpty() return remoteDigest.isEmpty() &#124;&#124; remoteDigest == BackupPaths.sha256(Files.readAllBytes(local))</code> | Merge can adopt remote epubDigest before bytes arrive, violating the local-digest assertion. Android’s later sync compares remote digest with a freshly hashed actual local file, so the permanent missed-download failure iOS fixed is avoided. SyncRepositoryTest.aCorrectedBookOfTheSameLengthIsStillFetched covers that safety, not honest stored digest. |
| `aLibraryThatAlreadyMigratedStillGetsDigests`<br>A Library That Already Migrated Still Gets Digests. | `KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:155` <code>#expect(work.epubDigest.isEmpty)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:163` <code>#expect(work.epubDigest == Storage.fileDigest(at: work.fileURL))</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:282–286` <code>// The digest of the bytes this device uploads (iOS // `Storage.fileDigest`). Android keeps none of its own, and the // one it carries from another device's manifest can describe an // older copy: a sync-down that trusted it would skip a changed book. epubDigests[BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(work.id)] = BackupPaths.sha256(bytes)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/SyncRepository.kt:649–653` <code>private fun isUnchangedLocally(work: BackupWork, remote: DocumentFile): Boolean { val local = workFileStore.workEpubPath(work.id) if (!Files.isRegularFile(local) &#124;&#124; Files.size(local) != remote.length()) return false val remoteDigest = work.epubDigest.orEmpty() return remoteDigest.isEmpty() &#124;&#124; remoteDigest == BackupPaths.sha256(Files.readAllBytes(local))</code> | Android deliberately computes hashes from files at sync comparison/publication rather than migrating a stored per-file digest. There is no Apple PersistenceMigrationService digest-backfill feature. SyncRepositoryTest.syncUpWritesTheDigestOfTheEpubItUploads. |
| `reImportingOverAFreedBookRefreshesItsDigest`<br>Re Importing Over AFreed Book Refreshes Its Digest. | `KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:176` <code>#expect(mine.count == corrected.count)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:198` <code>Issue.record("Expected the freed work to be restored, got \(outcome)")</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:202` <code>#expect(work.hasEPUB)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:203` <code>#expect(work.epubDigest == (try digest(of: corrected)))</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkImporter.kt:265–290` <code>if (existing != null) { if (!existing.hasEpub &#124;&#124; !fileStore.workEpubExists(existing.id)) { when (val write = fileStore.writeWorkEpub(existing.id, finalBytes)) { is FileWriteResult.Failure -&gt; return WorkImportResult.Failure( existing, AO3Error.Validation(write.message) ) is FileWriteResult.Success -&gt; Unit } } val restored = if (existing.isDeleted) { workRepository.restoreFromRecentlyDeleted(existing.id) ?: existing } else { existing } work = epubMetadata.applyTo( restored.copy( isSaved = true, hasEpub = true, remoteEpubPending = false, downloadedAt = restored.downloadedAt ?: downloadedAt, lastModifiedAt = workRepository.currentInstant() ), fillOnly = true ).withImportedAo3Ids() return WorkImportResult.Success(workRepository.upsert(work))</code> | Freed-record reimport restores bytes/pending state but copies the old epubDigest unchanged; stored digest can describe the old copy. Sync recomputes actual bytes, preventing that stale field from suppressing same-size correction there. No exact reimport digest test located. |
| `freeingAnEPUBCancelsAPromisedRemoteCopy`<br>Freeing An EPUBCancels APromised Remote Copy. | `KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:221` <code>#expect(!work.remoteEPUBPending)</code><br>`KudosTests/EPUBDigestHonestyTests.swift:226` <code>#expect(contents.manifest.works.first?.hasEPUB == false)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:382–385` <code>suspend fun deleteLocalEpub(workId: String): SavedWork? { val work = getWork(workId) ?: return null fileStore.deleteWorkEpub(workId) return upsert(work.copy(hasEpub = false, remoteEpubPending = false, freedAt = null, lastModifiedAt = clock()))</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:130–136` <code>isFavorite = isFavorite, hasGivenKudos = hasGivenKudos, isSaved = isSaved, isQueuedForLater = isQueuedForLater, isFinished = isFinished, hasEPUB = hasEpub &#124;&#124; remoteEpubPending, isComplete = isComplete,</code> | Explicit file removal clears pending; subsequent manifest no longer promises bytes. SyncRepositoryTest.aRemovedDownloadStaysRemovedUnlessAnotherManifestPromisesIt. |

## WorkDownloadTests

| Test — one-line guarantee | iOS assertion | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Reader consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `onlyAKeptCopyOffersRemove`<br>Only AKept Copy Offers Remove. | `KudosTests/WorkDownloadTests.swift:42` <code>#expect(WorkDownload.action(for: readingCopy) == .download)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkDownloadTests.swift:43` <code>#expect(!readingCopy.isDownloaded)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkDownloadTests.swift:45` <code>#expect(WorkDownload.action(for: readingCopy) == .removeDownload)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkDownloadTests.swift:46` <code>#expect(readingCopy.isDownloaded)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkDownloadTests.swift:49` <code>#expect(WorkDownload.action(for: missing) == .download)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/WorkDownloadSemantics.kt:11–28` <code>fun isDownloaded( hasEpub: Boolean, isSaved: Boolean, isKeptOffline: Boolean, hasAo3WorkId: Boolean ): Boolean = hasEpub &amp;&amp; (isSaved &#124;&#124; isKeptOffline &#124;&#124; !hasAo3WorkId) fun action( hasEpub: Boolean, isDownloaded: Boolean, hasAo3WorkId: Boolean, keptBy: String? ): WorkDownloadAction? = when { !hasAo3WorkId -&gt; null !hasEpub -&gt; WorkDownloadAction.Download keptBy != null -&gt; WorkDownloadAction.KeptBy(keptBy) isDownloaded -&gt; WorkDownloadAction.RemoveDownload else -&gt; WorkDownloadAction.Download</code> | WorkDownloadSemanticsTest.downloadedMeansAFileTheReaderChoseToKeep / actionsMatchIosKeepRules. |
