| `aCancelledSweepStopsAfterTheFetchInFlight`<br>A Cancelled Sweep Stops After The Fetch In Flight. | `KudosTests/WorkUpdateCheckerTests.swift:56` <code>#expect(await counter.count == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkUpdateCheckerTests.swift:57` <code>#expect((works[0].lastUpdateCheck != nil) == !fetchThrows)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkUpdateCheckerTests.swift:58` <code>#expect(works[1].lastUpdateCheck == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkUpdateCheckerTests.swift:59` <code>#expect(works[2].lastUpdateCheck == nil)</code> | **unsure** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkUpdateChecker.kt:32–53` <code>val now = clock() for (work in due) { val workId = WorkTags.ao3WorkIdFromUrl(work.sourceUrl) ?: continue when (val result = metadataRepository.fetch(workId)) { is AO3Result.Failure -&gt; { // Network / parse / locked page — keep what we have, but still // stamp the check time so a failing work backs off for MinInterval // like a successful one, instead of retrying on every Home open. workRepository.upsert(work.copy(lastUpdateCheck = now)) } is AO3Result.Success -&gt; { val chapters = result.value.chapters if (chapters.isBlank()) { // Fetched fine but nothing usable came back (e.g. a locked or // restricted work) — still stamp the check, same reasoning as // the Failure branch above. workRepository.upsert(work.copy(lastUpdateCheck = now)) } else { workRepository.upsert(applyChapterUpdate(work, chapters, now)) } } }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/network/ao3/work/AO3WorkMetadataRepository.kt:21–28` <code>is AO3Result.Success -&gt; { try { AO3Result.Success(withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { parser.parse(result.value.body, username) }) } catch (error: AO3WorkMetadataParseException.Overloaded) { AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Overloaded(result.value.statusCode, null)) } catch (error: Exception) { AO3Result.Failure(AO3Error.Parse(error.message ?: "AO3 work metadata could not be parsed.")) }</code> | No explicit loop cancellation check; cancellation during parsing is caught as Exception/Parse. Normal network cancellation propagates, and Room suspensions may abort, but the specific cancelled-fetch-returning-success/failure stamp behavior is not proved. No equivalent cancellation regression test located. |

WorkAvailabilitySweepTest.contactsOldestCheckedFirstAndNullsFirst covers ordering, not full exclusion tally. |

`PersistenceGateSuites.swift:13–14`: `@Suite(.serialized) enum PersistenceGateSuites {}` is recursive Swift Testing serialization, not a runtime gate test. Android uses JUnit/coroutines rather than this suite wrapper; runtime persistence gating is assessed in the restore/sync rows.

## The missing ones worth fixing first

*(Codex stopped at its usage limit before writing this list; ranked by Claude, 2026-10-15
17:40, from the 70 missing rows above.)*

1. The client requests any host it is given (lookalike, foreign, cleartext, loopback); the
   session cookie is already AO3-only (`AO3CookieJar`), so no session leaks. Brief 3fk.
2. Unparseable blurbs read as an empty result, not an error. Brief 3fk.
3. Non-identity cookies are persisted and reinstalled; sign-out may leave path- or
   subdomain-scoped cookies in the WebView. Brief 3fk.
4. Permanent delete leaves an imported original and its conversion record: **fixed in 3fj**.
5. A restore can switch off the delete confirmation: **fixed in 3fj**.
6. Hostile EPUB entries (unsafe or duplicate names, oversize, ratio, aggregate) pass preflight;
   Android does not extract EPUBs, so this is a zip-bomb guard. Brief 3fk.
7. Blank usernames build `/users//…`; availability checks (a 404 on refresh, a recovered ID,
   an inconclusive count); gift recipients and work-page status fields. Brief 3fk.
8. The document converter (imported HTML, text, PDF): one chapter, no metadata or word count.
   A feature to build, not a fault; recorded.


## Coverage and stopping point

Every listed iOS file was read (146 kept, 70 missing, 57 unsure); Codex stopped at its usage
limit while writing these two summary sections.
